- **ml-solution-design**: Turn customer requirements into an ML solution architecture. - **ml-specialist**: Entry point for all ML work. Runs the 10-stage ML Discipline Framework (FRAMEWORK.md) with HITL gates. Coordinates aiml-specialist (S4-S5) and ml-ops-specialist (S8-S9). - **mma-audit**: Use when the user asks to audit a spec / plan / design doc / skill file.
